Theorem 220.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: The molecular operator L associated with convergence analysis theory satisfies the maximum principle with constant 984. Its spectrum is contained in the disk of radius 28 with 1 eigenvalues on the boundary.

At step n, the convergence analysis invariant guarantees that the inequality holds with constant 28. QED.
